824 entries, design-thinking to design-web-audit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-thinking**: Guide any team through the Design Thinking methodology — empathize, define, ideate, prototype, test, and implement. Core audiences: product & design teams (product designers, UX researchers, UI designers, service designers, product managers), entrepreneurs & startups (idea validation, customer discovery, lean prototyping), and business & strategy leaders (innovation workshops, service redesign, customer journey mapping, business model innovation). Trigger on: design sprint, HMW question, how might we, empathy map, affinity diagram, point of view statement, design brief, user journey, service… ([rastian/design-thinking-skills](https://github.com/rastian/design-thinking-skills/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★20)
- **design-thinking**: Use when solve complex problems through empathy, ideation, prototyping, and testing. Use when tackling ambiguous challenges with user-centered approaches. ([oyi77/1ai-skills](https://github.com/oyi77/1ai-skills/tree/HEAD/mindset/design-thinking) · ★19)
- **design-thinking**: Activate when: user asks 'why isn't anyone using our product?', 'how do we understand what customers really want?', 'we know the tech works but the market isn't responding', team is stuck on a single solution and needs divergent ideas, or someone discusses user research / personas / prototyping / iterative design. Do NOT activate when: the problem and solution are both already validated and the challenge is pure execution; or the team cannot access real users at any point. More: deciqai.com/s/design-thinking ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/design-thinking) · ★16)
- **design-thinking**: Entry point for the 5-phase design thinking process. Assesses current state and routes to the appropriate phase skill. - **design-thinking**: Design Thinking: the code method. X → Graph → Effect&lt;A, E, R&gt;, the §1-§10 pipeline: shapes, happy path (A), cardinality, break points (E: ⟳ ↯ ☠), requirements (R), boundaries, behavior layers, scope, test layers, code.
